
====================================================================================================
001. _parse_sies_codigo_unico
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 27-35
REFERENCIAS: CODIGO_UNICO
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: _cargar_matriz_desambiguacion_desde_duracion
====================================================================================================
000027: def _parse_sies_codigo_unico(codigo_unico: object) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
000028:     text = str(codigo_unico or "").strip().upper()
000029:     m = re.match(r"^I\d+S\d+C\d+J(?P<jor>\d+)V(?P<ver>\d+)$", text)
000030:     if not m:
000031:         return (None, None)
000032:     j = m.group("jor")
000033:     jornada = {"1": "D", "2": "V", "3": "O", "4": "O"}.get(j)
000034:     version = f"V{m.group('ver')}"
000035:     return (jornada, version)

====================================================================================================
002. _split_codcarpr_candidates
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 38-49
REFERENCIAS: CODCARPR
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: _build_catalog_and_bridge_from_duracion | _cargar_matriz_desambiguacion_desde_duracion
====================================================================================================
000038: def _split_codcarpr_candidates(canon: object, aliases: object) -> list[str]:
000039:     out: set[str] = set()
000040:     canon_txt = str(canon or "").strip().upper()
000041:     if canon_txt:
000042:         out.add(canon_txt)
000043:     alias_txt = str(aliases or "").strip().upper()
000044:     if alias_txt:
000045:         for val in alias_txt.split("|"):
000046:             val = val.strip()
000047:             if val:
000048:                 out.add(val)
000049:     return sorted(out)

003. _load_duracion_as_governance_df
CLASIFICACIÓN: CATALOGOS_Y_PUENTES
LÍNEAS: 52-65
REFERENCIAS: CODIGO_UNICO | DURACION_ESTUDIOS
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: _build_catalog_and_bridge_from_duracion | _cargar_matriz_desambiguacion_desde_duracion
====================================================================================================
000052: def _load_duracion_as_governance_df() -> pd.DataFrame:
000053:     candidates = [
000054:         Path(__file__).with_name("DURACION_ESTUDIOS.tsv"),
000055:         Path.cwd() / "DURACION_ESTUDIOS.tsv",
000056:     ]
000057:     for path in candidates:
000058:         if path.exists():
000059:             try:
000060:                 df = pd.read_csv(path, sep="\t", dtype=str, keep_default_na=False)
000061:                 if "CODIGO_UNICO" in df.columns:
000062:                     return df
000063:             except Exception:
000064:                 continue
000065:     return pd.DataFrame()

====================================================================================================
004. _cargar_matriz_desambiguacion_desde_duracion
CLASIFICACIÓN: CATALOGOS_Y_PUENTES
LÍNEAS: 68-96
REFERENCIAS: CODCARPR | CODIGO_UNICO | DURACION_ESTUDIOS
LLAMA A: _load_duracion_as_governance_df | _parse_sies_codigo_unico | _split_codcarpr_candidates
LLAMADA DESDE: NINGUNA
====================================================================================================
000068: def _cargar_matriz_desambiguacion_desde_duracion() -> dict:
000069:     """Construye matriz (CODCARPR, JORNADA, VERSION)->SIES desde DURACION_ESTUDIOS.tsv."""
000070:     dur = _load_duracion_as_governance_df()
000071:     matriz_dict: dict[tuple[str, str, str], tuple[str, str, str]] = {}
000072:     if dur.empty:
000073:         print("⚠️  DURACION_ESTUDIOS.tsv no disponible para construir matriz SIES")
000074:         return matriz_dict
000075: 
000076:     conflictos = 0
000077:     for row in dur.itertuples(index=False):
000078:         jornada, version = _parse_sies_codigo_unico(getattr(row, "CODIGO_UNICO", ""))
000079:         if not jornada or not version:
000080:             continue
000081:         codcarprs = _split_codcarpr_candidates(
000082:             getattr(row, "CODCARPR_CANONICO", ""),
000083:             getattr(row, "CODCARPR_ALIAS_LIST", ""),
000084:         )
000085:         for codcarpr in codcarprs:
000086:             key = (codcarpr, jornada, version)
000087:             val = (getattr(row, "CODIGO_UNICO", ""), "100%", "AUTO_DURACION_ESTUDIOS")
000088:             if key in matriz_dict and matriz_dict[key][0] != val[0]:
000089:                 conflictos += 1
000090:                 continue
000091:             matriz_dict[key] = val
000092: 
000093:     if conflictos:
000094:         print(f"⚠️  Conflictos en matriz auto desde DURACION_ESTUDIOS: {conflictos} (se conserva primera ocurrencia)")
000095:     print(f"✅ Matriz SIES auto construida desde DURACION_ESTUDIOS: {len(matriz_dict)} llaves")
000096:     return matriz_dict

005. _first_existing_path
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA
LÍNEAS: 204-209
REFERENCIAS: NINGUNA
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: _default_input_arg | _load_for_ing_act_catalog | _resolve_optional_path | ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
000204: def _first_existing_path(candidates: list[Path]) -> Path | None:
000205:     for candidate in candidates:
000206:         candidate = candidate.expanduser()
000207:         if candidate.exists():
000208:             return candidate
000209:     return None

000258: def _pick_sheet(book: dict[str, pd.DataFrame], required: Iterable[str]) -> pd.DataFrame:
000259:     req = set(required)
000260:     for df in book.values():

000266: def _series_or_default(df: pd.DataFrame, col: str, default: str = "") -> pd.Series:

000269:     return pd.Series([default] * len(df), index=df.index, dtype=str)

011. _normalize_text
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA
LÍNEAS: 272-278
REFERENCIAS: NINGUNA
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: _build_key_3 | _build_key_no_jornada | _extract_alpha_prefix | _infer_codcarpr_from_codcli | _is_diplomado_name | _lookup_previous_origin_for_row | _normalize_continuidad_name_for_sies | _normalize_sexo_mu | _resolve_for_ing_act_row | ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
000272: def _normalize_text(value: object) -> str:
000273:     if pd.isna(value):
000274:         return ""
000275:     text = str(value).strip().upper()
000276:     text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text)
000277:     text = "".join(ch for ch in text if not unicodedata.combining(ch))
000278:     return re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text)

013. _infer_year_from_codcli
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 292-300
REFERENCIAS: CODCLI
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: NINGUNA
====================================================================================================
000292: def _infer_year_from_codcli(value: object) -> float:
000293:     if pd.isna(value):
000294:         return float("nan")
000295:     text = re.sub(r"\D", "", str(value))
000296:     if len(text) >= 4:
000297:         year = int(text[:4])
000298:         if 1900 <= year <= 2100:
000299:             return float(year)
000300:     return float("nan")

====================================================================================================
014. _infer_sem_from_codcli
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 303-314
REFERENCIAS: CODCLI
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: NINGUNA
====================================================================================================
000303: def _infer_sem_from_codcli(value: object) -> int | None:
000304:     if pd.isna(value):
000305:         return None
000306:     text = re.sub(r"[^A-Z0-9]", "", str(value).upper())
000307:     if len(text) < 5 or not text[:4].isdigit() or not text[4].isdigit():
000308:         return None
000309:     periodo = int(text[4])
000310:     if periodo == 1:
000311:         return 1
000312:     if periodo in (2, 3):
000313:         return 2
000314:     return None

====================================================================================================
015. _infer_codcarpr_from_codcli
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 317-328
REFERENCIAS: CODCARPR | CODCLI
LLAMA A: _normalize_text
LLAMADA DESDE: NINGUNA
====================================================================================================
000317: def _infer_codcarpr_from_codcli(value: object) -> str:
000318:     if pd.isna(value):
000319:         return ""
000320:     text = _normalize_text(value)
000321:     text = re.sub(r"[^A-Z0-9]", "", text)
000322:     # Estructura esperada CODCLI: YYYY + periodo(1 dígito) + CODCARPR + correlativo(3 dígitos)
000323:     m = re.match(r"^\d{4}\d(?P<cod>[A-Z0-9]+)\d{3}$", text)
000324:     if not m:
000325:         return ""
000326:     cod = m.group("cod")
000327:     # Guardrail: CODCARPR debe contener al menos una letra.
000328:     return cod if re.search(r"[A-Z]", cod) else ""

000331: def _pick_first_column(df: pd.DataFrame, options: list[str]) -> str | None:
000332:     upper_map = {c.upper(): c for c in df.columns}
000333:     for opt in options:
000334:         if opt.upper() in upper_map:
000335:             return upper_map[opt.upper()]
000336:     return None

018. _load_for_ing_act_catalog
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA

000345: def _load_for_ing_act_catalog() -> tuple[set[int], str]:
000346:     path = _first_existing_path(DEFAULT_GOB_FOR_ING_ACT_CANDIDATES)
000347:     if path is not None:
000348:         df = _load_governance_tsv(str(path), ["FOR_ING_ACT", "DESCRIPCION_MANUAL"])
000349:         if not df.empty:
000350:             codes = set(pd.to_numeric(df["FOR_ING_ACT"], errors="coerce").dropna().astype(int).tolist())
000351:             if codes:
000352:                 return codes, str(path)
000353:     return set(range(1, 12)), "fallback:hardcoded_1_11"

====================================================================================================
019. _normalize_semester_scalar
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA
LÍNEAS: 356-367
REFERENCIAS: NINGUNA
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: _lookup_previous_origin_for_row | _sem_sort_value
====================================================================================================
000356: def _normalize_semester_scalar(value: object, allow_zero: bool = False) -> int | None:
000357:     num = pd.to_numeric(pd.Series([value]), errors="coerce").iloc[0]
000358:     if pd.isna(num):
000359:         return None
000360:     sem = int(num)
000361:     if allow_zero and sem == 0:
000362:         return 0
000363:     if sem == 1:
000364:         return 1
000365:     if sem in (2, 3):
000366:         return 2
000367:     return None

020. _load_da_origin_records
CLASIFICACIÓN: LECTURA_PROMEDIOS
LÍNEAS: 370-410
REFERENCIAS: CODCARPR | CODCLI | DatosAlumnos
LLAMA A: _sem_sort_value
LLAMADA DESDE: _apply_for_ing_act_origin_rules
====================================================================================================
000370: def _load_da_origin_records(input_file: Path) -> dict[int, list[dict[str, object]]]:
000371:     """Carga registros mínimos de DatosAlumnos para buscar origen previo por RUT."""
000372:     try:
000373:         xls = pd.ExcelFile(input_file)
000374:         if "DatosAlumnos" not in xls.sheet_names:
000375:             return {}
000376:         keep = {"CODCLI", "RUT", "CODCARPR", "ANOINGRESO", "PERIODOINGRESO"}
000377:         src = pd.read_excel(input_file, sheet_name="DatosAlumnos", usecols=lambda c: c in keep)
000378:         required = {"CODCLI", "RUT", "ANOINGRESO"}
000379:         if not required.issubset(src.columns):
000380:             return {}
000381:         work = src.copy()
000382:         work["_RUT_NUM"] = work["RUT"].map(_rut_num_only)
000383:         work["_CODCLI"] = work["CODCLI"].astype(str).str.strip()
000384:         work["_CODCARPR"] = (
000385:             work["CODCARPR"].map(_normalize_text)
000386:             if "CODCARPR" in work.columns
000387:             else pd.Series("", index=work.index, dtype="object")
000388:         )
000389:         work["_ANIO"] = pd.to_numeric(work["ANOINGRESO"], errors="coerce")
000390:         work["_SEM"] = (
000391:             work["PERIODOINGRESO"].map(_normalize_semester_scalar)
000392:             if "PERIODOINGRESO" in work.columns
000393:             else pd.Series([None] * len(work), index=work.index, dtype="object")
000394:         )
000395:         work = work[work["_RUT_NUM"].notna() & work["_ANIO"].notna() & work["_CODCLI"].ne("")]
000396:         if work.empty:
000397:             return {}
000398:         work["_RUT_NUM"] = work["_RUT_NUM"].astype(int)
000399:         work["_ANIO"] = work["_ANIO"].astype(int)
000400:         def _sem_sort_value(value: object) -> int:
000401:             sem = _normalize_semester_scalar(value)
000402:             return sem if sem in (1, 2) else 9
000403:         records_by_rut: dict[int, list[dict[str, object]]] = {}
000404:         for rut, grp in work.groupby("_RUT_NUM", dropna=True):
000405:             records = grp[["_CODCLI", "_CODCARPR", "_ANIO", "_SEM"]].drop_duplicates().to_dict("records")
000406:             records.sort(key=lambda r: (int(r["_ANIO"]), _sem_sort_value(r["_SEM"]), str(r["_CODCLI"])))
000407:             records_by_rut[int(rut)] = records
000408:         return records_by_rut
000409:     except Exception:
000410:         return {}

000401:             sem = _normalize_semester_scalar(value)
000402:             return sem if sem in (1, 2) else 9

022. _load_for_ing_act_tns_origin_trace
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 413-446
REFERENCIAS: CODCLI
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: _apply_for_ing_act_origin_rules
====================================================================================================
000413: def _load_for_ing_act_tns_origin_trace(trace_path: Path) -> dict[int, tuple[int | None, int | None]]:
000414:     if not trace_path.exists():
000415:         return {}
000416:     try:
000417:         header = pd.read_csv(trace_path, sep="\t", nrows=0)
000418:         needed = {"_RUT_NUM", "FOR_ING_ACT"}
000419:         if not needed.issubset(header.columns):
000420:             return {}
000421:         optional = ["TNS_PREV_MIN_ANO_DA", "TNS_PREV_CODCLI_EJEMPLO_DA"]
000422:         usecols = ["_RUT_NUM", "FOR_ING_ACT"] + [c for c in optional if c in header.columns]
000423:         trace = pd.read_csv(trace_path, sep="\t", usecols=usecols)
000424:         trace["FOR_ING_ACT"] = pd.to_numeric(trace["FOR_ING_ACT"], errors="coerce")
000425:         trace["_RUT_NUM"] = pd.to_numeric(trace["_RUT_NUM"], errors="coerce")
000426:         trace = trace[trace["FOR_ING_ACT"].eq(11) & trace["_RUT_NUM"].notna()].copy()
000427:         if trace.empty:
000428:             return {}
000429:         if "TNS_PREV_MIN_ANO_DA" in trace.columns:
000430:             trace["_TNS_ANIO"] = pd.to_numeric(trace["TNS_PREV_MIN_ANO_DA"], errors="coerce")
000431:         else:
000432:             trace["_TNS_ANIO"] = pd.NA
000433:         if "TNS_PREV_CODCLI_EJEMPLO_DA" in trace.columns:
000434:             trace["_TNS_SEM"] = trace["TNS_PREV_CODCLI_EJEMPLO_DA"].map(_infer_sem_from_codcli)
000435:         else:
000436:             trace["_TNS_SEM"] = None
000437:         out: dict[int, tuple[int | None, int | None]] = {}
000438:         for rut, grp in trace.groupby(trace["_RUT_NUM"].astype(int)):
000439:             years = pd.to_numeric(grp["_TNS_ANIO"], errors="coerce").dropna()
000440:             year = int(years.min()) if not years.empty else None
000441:             sem_values = [sem for sem in grp["_TNS_SEM"].tolist() if sem in (1, 2)]
000442:             sem = sem_values[0] if sem_values else None
000443:             out[int(rut)] = (year, sem)
000444:         return out
000445:     except Exception:
000446:         return {}

====================================================================================================
023. _lookup_previous_origin_for_row
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 449-491
REFERENCIAS: CODCARPR | CODCLI
LLAMA A: _normalize_semester_scalar | _normalize_text
LLAMADA DESDE: _apply_for_ing_act_origin_rules
====================================================================================================
000449: def _lookup_previous_origin_for_row(
000450:     records_by_rut: dict[int, list[dict[str, object]]],
000451:     rut_num: int | None,
000452:     codcli_actual: object,
000453:     codcarpr_actual: object,
000454:     anio_act: object,
000455:     sem_act: object,
000456: ) -> tuple[int | None, int | None]:
000457:     if rut_num is None:
000458:         return None, None
000459:     records = records_by_rut.get(int(rut_num), [])
000460:     if not records:
000461:         return None, None
000462:     current_codcli = str(codcli_actual).strip()
000463:     current_codcarpr = _normalize_text(codcarpr_actual)
000464:     current_year_num = pd.to_numeric(pd.Series([anio_act]), errors="coerce").iloc[0]
000465:     current_sem = _normalize_semester_scalar(sem_act, allow_zero=True)
000466:     current_tuple = None
000467:     if not pd.isna(current_year_num):
000468:         current_tuple = (int(current_year_num), current_sem if current_sem is not None else 9)
000469: 
000470:     candidates: list[tuple[int, int | None]] = []
000471:     for rec in records:
000472:         rec_codcli = str(rec.get("_CODCLI", "")).strip()
000473:         if rec_codcli and rec_codcli == current_codcli:
000474:             continue
000475:         rec_codcarpr = _normalize_text(rec.get("_CODCARPR", ""))
000476:         if current_codcarpr and rec_codcarpr and rec_codcarpr == current_codcarpr:
000477:             continue
000478:         rec_year = rec.get("_ANIO")
000479:         if rec_year is None or pd.isna(rec_year):
000480:             continue
000481:         rec_sem = rec.get("_SEM")
000482:         rec_sem_norm = _normalize_semester_scalar(rec_sem)
000483:         rec_tuple = (int(rec_year), rec_sem_norm if rec_sem_norm in (1, 2) else 9)
000484:         if current_tuple is not None and rec_tuple >= current_tuple:
000485:             continue
000486:         candidates.append((int(rec_year), rec_sem_norm if rec_sem_norm in (1, 2) else None))
000487: 
000488:     if not candidates:
000489:         return None, None
000490:     candidates.sort(key=lambda item: (item[0], item[1] if item[1] is not None else 9))
000491:     return candidates[0]

024. _apply_for_ing_act_origin_rules

027. _normalize_period_to_semester
CLASIFICACIÓN: LECTURA_PROMEDIOS
LÍNEAS: 615-621
REFERENCIAS: Hoja1
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: _build_mu_historico_summary
====================================================================================================
000615: def _normalize_period_to_semester(values: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
000616:     periodo = pd.to_numeric(values, errors="coerce")
000617:     sem = pd.Series(pd.NA, index=values.index, dtype="Int64")
000618:     sem.loc[periodo == 1] = 1
000619:     # El histórico Hoja1 usa PERIODO=3 como equivalente operacional de segundo semestre.
000620:     sem.loc[periodo.isin([2, 3])] = 2
000621:     return sem

====================================================================================================
028. _trimester_level_to_semester
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA
LÍNEAS: 624-641
REFERENCIAS: NINGUNA
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
000624: def _trimester_level_to_semester(values: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
000625:     """Convierte nivel administrativo trimestral a semestre curricular MU.
000626: 
000627:     Regla institucional inmutable:
000628:     1→1, 2→2, 3→2, 4→3, 5→4, 6→4, 7→5, 8→6, 9→6, 10→7, 11→8, 12→8.
000629:     Fallback para niveles >12: ceil(n*2/3), equivalente entero ((2n+2)//3).
000630:     """
000631:     nivel = pd.to_numeric(values, errors="coerce").round()
000632:     map_eq = {
000633:         1: 1, 2: 2, 3: 2,
000634:         4: 3, 5: 4, 6: 4,
000635:         7: 5, 8: 6, 9: 6,
000636:         10: 7, 11: 8, 12: 8,
000637:     }
000638:     sem = nivel.map(map_eq)
000639:     fallback_mask = sem.isna() & nivel.notna() & nivel.gt(12)
000640:     sem.loc[fallback_mask] = ((nivel.loc[fallback_mask] * 2 + 2) // 3)
000641:     return sem.astype("Float64")

====================================================================================================
029. _normalize_grade_to_mu_scale
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA
LÍNEAS: 644-655
REFERENCIAS: NINGUNA
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: _build_mu_historico_summary
====================================================================================================
000644: def _normalize_grade_to_mu_scale(values: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
000645:     # Escala fuente → escala MU (100-700), documentada en gobernanza_escala_notas.tsv
000646:     # Fuente 1.0-7.0  → multiplicar por 100 (ej. 5.8 → 580)
000647:     # Fuente 10-70    → multiplicar por 10  (ej. 58 → 580)
000648:     # Fuente 100-700  → ya en escala, usar directamente
000649:     # Fuente 0 o fuera de rango → NA (sin calificacion; no contamina promedios)
000650:     nota = pd.to_numeric(values, errors="coerce")
000651:     out = pd.Series(pd.NA, index=values.index, dtype="Float64")
000652:     out.loc[(nota >= 1) & (nota <= 7)] = (nota.loc[(nota >= 1) & (nota <= 7)] * 100).round()
000653:     out.loc[(nota > 7) & (nota <= 70)] = (nota.loc[(nota > 7) & (nota <= 70)] * 10).round()
000654:     out.loc[(nota >= 100) & (nota <= 700)] = nota.loc[(nota >= 100) & (nota <= 700)].round()
000655:     return out.astype("Int64")

====================================================================================================
030. _coerce_mu_average
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA
LÍNEAS: 658-665
REFERENCIAS: NINGUNA
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: _build_mu_historico_summary
====================================================================================================
000658: def _coerce_mu_average(series: pd.Series) -> int:
000659:     nota = pd.to_numeric(series, errors="coerce").dropna()
000660:     if nota.empty:
000661:         return 0
000662:     avg = int(round(float(nota.mean())))
000663:     if avg == 0:
000664:         return 0
000665:     return min(max(avg, 100), 700)

====================================================================================================
031. _build_mu_historico_summary
CLASIFICACIÓN: LECTURA_PROMEDIOS
LÍNEAS: 668-753
REFERENCIAS: CODCARPR | Hoja1
LLAMA A: _coerce_mu_average | _normalize_doc | _normalize_grade_to_mu_scale | _normalize_period_to_semester | _series_or_default
LLAMADA DESDE: ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
000668: def _build_mu_historico_summary(
000669:     src: pd.DataFrame,
000670:     rut_col: str,
000671:     dv_col: str,
000672:     codcarr_col: str,
000673:     anio_ref_override: int | None = None,
000674: ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, int | None]:
000675:     required = {"ANO", "PERIODO", "CODRAMO", rut_col, dv_col, codcarr_col}
000676:     if not required.issubset(src.columns):
000677:         return pd.DataFrame(), None
000678: 
000679:     hist_cols = ["ANO", "PERIODO", "CODRAMO", rut_col, dv_col, codcarr_col]
000680:     extra_cols = [c for c in ["DESCRIPCION_ESTADO", "ESTADO", "CONVALIDADO", "NOTA_FINAL"] if c in src.columns]
000681:     hist = src[hist_cols + extra_cols].copy().rename(
000682:         columns={
000683:             rut_col: "RUT",
000684:             dv_col: "DIG",
000685:             codcarr_col: "CODCARR",
000686:         }
000687:     )
000688:     hist["RUT_NORM"] = [_normalize_doc(n, d) for n, d in zip(hist["RUT"], hist["DIG"])]
000689:     hist["CODCARPR_NORM"] = hist["CODCARR"].map(_normalize_text)
000690:     hist["ANO_NUM"] = pd.to_numeric(hist["ANO"], errors="coerce")
000691:     hist["SEMESTRE_HIST"] = _normalize_period_to_semester(hist["PERIODO"])
000692:     hist["NOTA_MU"] = _normalize_grade_to_mu_scale(hist["NOTA_FINAL"]) if "NOTA_FINAL" in hist.columns else pd.Series(pd.NA, index=hist.index, dtype="Int64")
000693:     hist["ESTADO_HIST_NORM"] = _series_or_default(hist, "DESCRIPCION_ESTADO").map(_normalize_text)
000694:     hist["CONVALIDADO_NORM"] = _series_or_default(hist, "CONVALIDADO").map(_normalize_text)
000695: 
000696:     anio_vals = hist["ANO_NUM"].dropna()
000697:     if anio_vals.empty:
000698:         return pd.DataFrame(), None
000699:     anio_ref_data = int(anio_vals.max())
000700:     # Si se proporciona override (anio_anterior_prom = ANIO_ING_ACT - 1), usarlo para PROM;
000701:     # de lo contrario, caer al max del histórico (comportamiento legacy).
000702:     anio_ref = anio_ref_override if anio_ref_override is not None else anio_ref_data
000703:     print(f"  [HIST] Año referencia PROM: {anio_ref} (max datos: {anio_ref_data}, override: {anio_ref_override})")
000704: 
000705:     rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
000706:     for (rut_norm, codcarpr_norm), sub in hist.groupby(["RUT_NORM", "CODCARPR_NORM"], dropna=False):
000707:         sub_ref = sub[sub["ANO_NUM"] == anio_ref].copy()
000708:         estado_ref = sub_ref["ESTADO_HIST_NORM"]
000709:         estado_hist = sub["ESTADO_HIST_NORM"]
000710:         transfer_ref = estado_ref.str.contains(r"CONVALID|HOMOLOG|RECONOC|EQUIV", regex=True, na=False) | sub_ref["CONVALIDADO_NORM"].eq("S")
000711:         graded_ref = sub_ref["NOTA_MU"].notna() & ~transfer_ref
000712:         sem_ref = sub_ref["SEMESTRE_HIST"]
000713:         aprob_ref = estado_ref.str.contains("APROB", na=False) & ~transfer_ref
000714:         aprob_hist = estado_hist.str.contains(r"APROB|CONVALID|RECONOC|EQUIV|HOMOLOG", regex=True, na=False)
000715:         anios_grupo = sorted({int(v) for v in sub["ANO_NUM"].dropna().astype(int).tolist()})
000716:         anio_min = anios_grupo[0] if anios_grupo else anio_ref
000717:         anio_max = anios_grupo[-1] if anios_grupo else anio_ref
000718:         anios_disponibles = len(anios_grupo)
000719:         hist_scope_status = "ALCANCE_MULTIANUAL" if anios_disponibles > 1 else "ALCANCE_LIMITADO_ANIO_UNICO"
000720: 
000721:         codramo_ref = sub_ref["CODRAMO"] if "CODRAMO" in sub_ref.columns else pd.Series(index=sub_ref.index, dtype=object)
000722:         codramo_hist = sub["CODRAMO"] if "CODRAMO" in sub.columns else pd.Series(index=sub.index, dtype=object)
000723: 
000724:         rows.append(
000725:             {
000726:                 "RUT_NORM": rut_norm,
000727:                 "CODCARPR_NORM": codcarpr_norm,
000728:                 "UZ_HIST_KEY": f"{rut_norm}|{codcarpr_norm}",
000729:                 "ANIO_REFERENCIA_HIST_UZ": anio_ref,
000730:                 "UZ_HIST_ANIO_MIN": anio_min,
000731:                 "UZ_HIST_ANIO_MAX": anio_max,
000732:                 "UZ_HIST_ANIOS_DISPONIBLES": anios_disponibles,
000733:                 "UZ_HIST_SCOPE_STATUS": hist_scope_status,
000734:                 "UZ_HIST_FILAS_TOTAL": int(len(sub)),
000735:                 "UZ_HIST_FILAS_ANIO_REFERENCIA": int(len(sub_ref)),
000736:                 "UZ_HIST_FILAS_REF_APROB": int(aprob_ref.sum()),
000737:                 "UZ_HIST_FILAS_REF_REPROB": int(estado_ref.str.contains("REPROB", na=False).sum()),
000738:                 "UZ_HIST_FILAS_REF_TRANSFER": int(transfer_ref.sum()),
000739:                 "UZ_HIST_FILAS_REF_SEM1_CALIFICADAS": int((graded_ref & sem_ref.eq(1)).sum()),
000740:                 "UZ_HIST_FILAS_REF_SEM2_CALIFICADAS": int((graded_ref & sem_ref.eq(2)).sum()),
000741:                 "ASI_INS_ANT_HIST": int(codramo_ref[~transfer_ref].nunique()),
000742:                 "ASI_APR_ANT_HIST": int(codramo_ref[aprob_ref].nunique()),
000743:                 "PROM_PRI_SEM_HIST": _coerce_mu_average(sub_ref.loc[graded_ref & sem_ref.eq(1), "NOTA_MU"]),
000744:                 "PROM_SEG_SEM_HIST": _coerce_mu_average(sub_ref.loc[graded_ref & sem_ref.eq(2), "NOTA_MU"]),
000745:                 "ASI_INS_HIS_HIST": int(codramo_hist.dropna().count()),
000746:                 "ASI_APR_HIS_HIST": int(codramo_hist[aprob_hist].nunique()),
000747:                 "UZ_FUENTE_HIST": f"HISTORICO_HOJA1_ANIO_{anio_ref}",
000748:             }
000749:         )
000750: 
000751:     if not rows:
000752:         return pd.DataFrame(), anio_ref_data
000753:     return pd.DataFrame(rows), anio_ref_data

====================================================================================================
032. _resolve_for_ing_act_row
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 756-898
REFERENCIAS: CODCARPR
LLAMA A: _normalize_text | _numeric_code
LLAMADA DESDE: ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
000756: def _resolve_for_ing_act_row(
000757:     raw_input_value: object,
000758:     vias_admision_value: object,
000759:     carrera_value: object,
000760:     codcarpr_value: object,
000761:     valid_codes: set[int],
000762: ) -> dict[str, object]:
000763:     def _numeric_code(value: object) -> int | None:
000764:         num = pd.to_numeric(pd.Series([value]), errors="coerce").iloc[0]
000765:         if pd.isna(num):
000766:             return None
000767:         num_int = int(num)
000768:         return num_int if num_int in valid_codes else None
000769: 
000770:     input_code = _numeric_code(raw_input_value)
000771:     if input_code is not None:
000772:         return {
000773:             "FOR_ING_ACT": input_code,
000774:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_VALOR": raw_input_value,
000775:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_CAMPO": "INPUT_FOR_ING_ACT_EQUIVALENTE",
000776:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_NORM": _normalize_text(raw_input_value),
000777:             "FOR_ING_ACT_METODO": "NUMERICO_EXACTO_INPUT",
000778:             "FOR_ING_ACT_IMPUTADO": "NO",
000779:             "FOR_ING_ACT_REQUIERE_REVISION": "NO",
000780:         }
000781: 
000782:     source_field = "SIN_FUENTE"
000783:     source_value = pd.NA
000784:     if not pd.isna(vias_admision_value) and str(vias_admision_value).strip():
000785:         source_field = "DA_VIASDEADMISION"
000786:         source_value = vias_admision_value
000787:     elif not pd.isna(raw_input_value) and str(raw_input_value).strip():
000788:         source_field = "INPUT_FOR_ING_ACT_EQUIVALENTE"
000789:         source_value = raw_input_value
000790: 
000791:     source_norm = _normalize_text(source_value)
000792:     carrera_norm = _normalize_text(carrera_value)
000793:     codcarpr_norm = _normalize_text(codcarpr_value)
000794: 
000795:     exact_map = {
000796:         "ENSENANZA MEDIA NACIONAL": 1,
000797:         "EXTRANJERO": 6,
000798:     }
000799:     if source_norm in exact_map and exact_map[source_norm] in valid_codes:
000800:         return {
000801:             "FOR_ING_ACT": exact_map[source_norm],
000802:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_VALOR": source_value,
000803:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_CAMPO": source_field,
000804:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_NORM": source_norm,
000805:             "FOR_ING_ACT_METODO": "CATALOGO_EXACTO_VIAS_ADMISION",
000806:             "FOR_ING_ACT_IMPUTADO": "NO",
000807:             "FOR_ING_ACT_REQUIERE_REVISION": "NO",
000808:         }
000809: 
000810:     token_rules = [
000811:         ("CAMBIO EXTERNO", 4, "TOKEN_CAMBIO_EXTERNO"),
000812:         ("CAMBIO INTERNO", 3, "TOKEN_CAMBIO_INTERNO"),
000813:         ("RECONOCIMIENTO DE APRENDIZAJES PREVIOS", 5, "TOKEN_RAP"),
000814:         ("RAP", 5, "TOKEN_RAP"),
000815:         ("PACE", 7, "TOKEN_PACE"),
000816:         ("INCLUSION", 8, "TOKEN_INCLUSION"),
000817:         ("PLAN COMUN", 2, "TOKEN_PLAN_COMUN"),
000818:         ("BACHILLER", 2, "TOKEN_BACHILLERATO"),
000819:         ("ARTICUL", 11, "TOKEN_ARTICULACION"),
000820:         ("EXTRANJER", 6, "TOKEN_EXTRANJERO"),
000821:         ("ENSENANZA MEDIA", 1, "TOKEN_INGRESO_DIRECTO"),
000822:     ]
000823:     for token, code, method in token_rules:
000824:         if token in source_norm and code in valid_codes:
000825:             return {
000826:                 "FOR_ING_ACT": code,
000827:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_VALOR": source_value,
000828:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_CAMPO": source_field,
000829:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_NORM": source_norm,
000830:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_METODO": method,
000831:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_IMPUTADO": "NO",
000832:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_REQUIERE_REVISION": "NO",
000833:             }
000834: 
000835:     if source_norm == "PROGRAMA DE EDUCACION CONTINUA" and 11 in valid_codes:
000836:         if "CONTINUIDAD" in carrera_norm or "ARTICUL" in carrera_norm or codcarpr_norm.startswith("CI"):
000837:             return {
000838:                 "FOR_ING_ACT": 11,
000839:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_VALOR": source_value,
000840:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_CAMPO": source_field,
000841:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_NORM": source_norm,
000842:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_METODO": "REGLA_CONTINUIDAD_ARTICULACION_CARRERA",
000843:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_IMPUTADO": "NO",
000844:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_REQUIERE_REVISION": "NO",
000845:             }
000846:         if 10 in valid_codes:
000847:             return {
000848:                 "FOR_ING_ACT": 10,
000849:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_VALOR": source_value,
000850:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_CAMPO": source_field,
000851:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_NORM": source_norm,
000852:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_METODO": "FALLBACK_CONTROLADO_PROGRAMA_EDUCACION_CONTINUA",
000853:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_IMPUTADO": "SI",
000854:                 "FOR_ING_ACT_REQUIERE_REVISION": "SI",
000855:             }
000856: 
000857:     if source_norm == "MNP AA" and 10 in valid_codes:
000858:         return {
000859:             "FOR_ING_ACT": 10,
000860:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_VALOR": source_value,
000861:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_CAMPO": source_field,
000862:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_NORM": source_norm,
000863:             "FOR_ING_ACT_METODO": "FALLBACK_CONTROLADO_MNP_AA",
000864:             "FOR_ING_ACT_IMPUTADO": "SI",
000865:             "FOR_ING_ACT_REQUIERE_REVISION": "SI",
000866:         }
000867: 
000868:     if source_field == "SIN_FUENTE":
000869:         return {
000870:             "FOR_ING_ACT": pd.NA,
000871:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_VALOR": pd.NA,
000872:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_CAMPO": source_field,
000873:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_NORM": "",
000874:             "FOR_ING_ACT_METODO": "SIN_FUENTE_FOR_ING_ACT",
000875:             "FOR_ING_ACT_IMPUTADO": "NO",
000876:             "FOR_ING_ACT_REQUIERE_REVISION": "SI",
000877:         }
000878: 
000879:     if 10 in valid_codes:
000880:         return {
000881:             "FOR_ING_ACT": 10,
000882:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_VALOR": source_value,
000883:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_CAMPO": source_field,
000884:             "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_NORM": source_norm,
000885:             "FOR_ING_ACT_METODO": "FALLBACK_CONTROLADO_OTRAS_FORMAS",
000886:             "FOR_ING_ACT_IMPUTADO": "SI",
000887:             "FOR_ING_ACT_REQUIERE_REVISION": "SI",
000888:         }

000892:         "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_VALOR": source_value,

033. _numeric_code

000763:     def _numeric_code(value: object) -> int | None:
000764:         num = pd.to_numeric(pd.Series([value]), errors="coerce").iloc[0]
000765:         if pd.isna(num):
000766:             return None
000767:         num_int = int(num)
000768:         return num_int if num_int in valid_codes else None

034. _build_for_ing_act_report_payload
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 901-1015
REFERENCIAS: CODCARPR | CODCLI
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
000901: def _build_for_ing_act_report_payload(
000902:     stage_df: pd.DataFrame,
000903:     included_mask: pd.Series,
000904:     valid_codes: set[int],
000905:     input_source_column: str | None,
000906:     catalog_source: str,
000907: ) -> dict[str, object]:
000908:     included_mask = included_mask.reindex(stage_df.index, fill_value=False).astype(bool)
000909:     included = stage_df.loc[included_mask].copy()
000910:     valid_codes_sorted = sorted(int(v) for v in valid_codes)
000911:     report: dict[str, object] = {
000912:         "source_used": {
000913:             "input_column_detected": input_source_column or "NO_ENCONTRADO",
000914:             "catalog_source": catalog_source or "NO_ENCONTRADO",
000915:             "valid_codes_catalog": valid_codes_sorted,
000916:         },
000917:         "rows_total_stage": int(len(stage_df)),
000918:         "rows_included_final": int(len(included)),
000919:         "resolved_ok": 0,
000920:         "unresolved": {
000921:             "count": 0,
000922:             "pct_rows_included": 0.0,
000923:             "reasons": {},
000924:             "continuidad_rule_fail_count": 0,
000925:         },
000926:         "distribution": {},
000927:         "invalid_examples": [],
000928:     }
000929:     if included.empty:
000930:         return report
000931: 
000932:     for_num = pd.to_numeric(included["FOR_ING_ACT"], errors="coerce")
000933:     for_valid_mask = for_num.isin(valid_codes_sorted)
000934:     for_notnull_mask = for_num.notna()
000935:     for_invalido_mask = ~(for_valid_mask & for_notnull_mask)
000936:     review_mask = included["FOR_ING_ACT_REQUIERE_REVISION"].astype(str).str.upper().eq("SI")
000937: 
000938:     continuidad_codes = {2, 3, 4, 5, 11}
000939:     continuidad_mask = for_num.isin(sorted(continuidad_codes))
000940:     anio_act = pd.to_numeric(included["ANIO_ING_ACT"], errors="coerce")
000941:     sem_act = pd.to_numeric(included["SEM_ING_ACT"], errors="coerce")
000942:     anio_ori = pd.to_numeric(included["ANIO_ING_ORI"], errors="coerce")
000943:     sem_ori = pd.to_numeric(included["SEM_ING_ORI"], errors="coerce")
000944:     continuidad_fail_mask = continuidad_mask & anio_act.eq(anio_ori) & sem_act.eq(sem_ori)
000945: 
000946:     unresolved_mask = for_invalido_mask | review_mask | continuidad_fail_mask
000947:     resolved_ok = int((~unresolved_mask).sum())
000948:     unresolved_count = int(unresolved_mask.sum())
000949: 
000950:     reasons: dict[str, int] = {
000951:         "for_ing_act_nulo_o_fuera_catalogo": int(for_invalido_mask.sum()),
000952:         "for_ing_act_requiere_revision": int(review_mask.sum()),
000953:         "anexo7_continuidad_origen_igual_actual": int(continuidad_fail_mask.sum()),
000954:     }
000955:     reasons = {k: int(v) for k, v in reasons.items() if int(v) > 0}
000956: 
000957:     fuentecampo_dist = (
000958:         included["FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_CAMPO"]
000959:         .fillna("SIN_DATO")
000960:         .astype(str)
000961:         .str.strip()
000962:         .replace("", "SIN_DATO")
000963:         .value_counts(dropna=False)
000964:         .to_dict()
000965:     )
000966:     metodo_dist = (
000967:         included["FOR_ING_ACT_METODO"]
000968:         .fillna("SIN_DATO")
000969:         .astype(str)
000970:         .str.strip()
000971:         .replace("", "SIN_DATO")
000972:         .value_counts(dropna=False)
000973:         .to_dict()
000974:     )
000975:     report["source_used"]["fuente_campo_distribution"] = {str(k): int(v) for k, v in fuentecampo_dist.items()}
000976:     report["source_used"]["metodo_distribution"] = {str(k): int(v) for k, v in metodo_dist.items()}
000977: 
000978:     for_dist = (
000979:         for_num.astype("Int64")
000980:         .astype(str)
000981:         .replace("<NA>", "NA")
000982:         .value_counts(dropna=False)
000983:         .to_dict()
000984:     )
000985: 
000986:     example_cols = [
000987:         "CODCLI",
000988:         "N_DOC",
000989:         "DV",
000990:         "CODCARPR_NORM",
000991:         "NOMBRE_CARRERA_FUENTE",
000992:         "FOR_ING_ACT",
000993:         "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_VALOR",
000994:         "FOR_ING_ACT_FUENTE_CAMPO",
000995:         "FOR_ING_ACT_METODO",
000996:         "FOR_ING_ACT_REQUIERE_REVISION",
000997:         "ANIO_ING_ACT",
000998:         "SEM_ING_ACT",
000999:         "ANIO_ING_ORI",
001000:         "SEM_ING_ORI",
001001:     ]
001002:     examples_df = included.loc[unresolved_mask, example_cols].head(25).copy()
001003:     examples_df.insert(0, "ROW_EXCEL_1_BASED", (examples_df.index + 2).astype(int))
001004:     examples_df = examples_df.fillna("").astype(str)
001005: 
001006:     report["resolved_ok"] = resolved_ok
001007:     report["unresolved"] = {
001008:         "count": unresolved_count,
001009:         "pct_rows_included": round((unresolved_count / len(included)) * 100, 2) if len(included) else 0.0,
001010:         "reasons": reasons,
001011:         "continuidad_rule_fail_count": int(continuidad_fail_mask.sum()),
001012:     }
001013:     report["distribution"] = {str(k): int(v) for k, v in for_dist.items()}
001014:     report["invalid_examples"] = examples_df.to_dict(orient="records")
001015:     return report

035. _map_jornada_to_mod_jor
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA
LÍNEAS: 1018-1042
REFERENCIAS: NINGUNA
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
001018: def _map_jornada_to_mod_jor(series: pd.Series) -> tuple[pd.Series, pd.Series]:
001019:     s_norm = series.fillna("").map(_normalize_text)
001020:     s_low = s_norm.str.lower()
001021:     modalidad = pd.Series(pd.NA, index=series.index, dtype="object")
001022:     jor = pd.Series(pd.NA, index=series.index, dtype="object")
001023: 
001024:     diurna = s_norm.isin({"D", "1", "DIURNA"}) | s_low.str.contains("diurn", na=False)
001025:     vespertina = s_norm.isin({"V", "2", "VESPERTINA"}) | s_low.str.contains("vespert", na=False)
001026:     semi = s_norm.isin({"3", "SEMIPRESENCIAL"}) | s_low.str.contains("semi", na=False)
001027:     distancia = (
001028:         s_norm.isin({"O", "4", "A DISTANCIA", "DISTANCIA", "ONLINE"})
001029:         | s_low.str.contains("dist", na=False)
001030:         | s_low.str.contains("online", na=False)
001031:     )
001032: 
001033:     modalidad.loc[diurna | vespertina] = "1"  # Presencial
001034:     modalidad.loc[semi] = "2"  # Semipresencial
001035:     modalidad.loc[distancia] = "3"  # No presencial
001036: 
001037:     jor.loc[diurna] = "1"
001038:     jor.loc[vespertina] = "2"
001039:     jor.loc[semi] = "3"
001040:     jor.loc[distancia] = "4"
001041: 
001042:     return modalidad, jor

036. _build_nombre_carrera_to_cod_car
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA
LÍNEAS: 1060-1081
REFERENCIAS: NINGUNA
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
001060: def _build_nombre_carrera_to_cod_car(oferta_dim: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[str, int]:
001061:     """Construye mapeo NOMBRE_CARRERA normalizado → CODIGO_CARRERA desde oferta/DURACION."""
001062:     mapping: dict[str, int] = {}
001063:     if oferta_dim.empty:
001064:         return mapping
001065:     for _, row in oferta_dim.iterrows():
001066:         nombre = str(row.get("NOMBRE_CARRERA", "")).strip().upper()
001067:         nombre = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", nombre)
001068:         cod_car = row.get("CODIGO_CARRERA")
001069:         if nombre and pd.notna(cod_car):
001070:             cod_car_int = int(cod_car)
001071:             if nombre not in mapping:
001072:                 mapping[nombre] = cod_car_int
001073:     # Alias de normalización conocidos (singular/plural, variantes)
001074:     _aliases: list[tuple[str, str]] = [
001075:         ("TECNICO EN ANALISIS DE SISTEMA", "TECNICO EN ANALISIS DE SISTEMAS"),
001076:         ("DIPLOMADO EN CIBERSEGURIDAD", "DIPLOMADO EN CIBERSEGURIDAD APLICADA"),
001077:     ]
001078:     for alias, canonical in _aliases:
001079:         if alias not in mapping and canonical in mapping:
001080:             mapping[alias] = mapping[canonical]
001081:     return mapping

037. _modalidad_from_jor
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA
LÍNEAS: 1107-1113
REFERENCIAS: NINGUNA
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
001107: def _modalidad_from_jor(jor_series: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
001108:     j = pd.to_numeric(jor_series, errors="coerce")
001109:     mod = pd.Series(pd.NA, index=jor_series.index, dtype="object")
001110:     mod.loc[j.isin([1, 2])] = "1"  # Presencial
001111:     mod.loc[j == 3] = "2"  # Semipresencial
001112:     mod.loc[j.isin([4, 5])] = "3"  # No presencial
001113:     return mod

038. _normalize_sexo_mu
CLASIFICACIÓN: LECTURA_PROMEDIOS
LÍNEAS: 1116-1134
REFERENCIAS: DatosAlumnos
LLAMA A: _normalize_text
LLAMADA DESDE: NINGUNA
====================================================================================================
001116: def _normalize_sexo_mu(value: object) -> object:
001117:     if pd.isna(value):
001118:         return pd.NA
001119:     raw = str(value).strip().upper()
001120:     # DatosAlumnos usa códigos institucionales M/F/S; el manual MU exige H/M/NB.
001121:     if raw == "M":
001122:         return "H"
001123:     if raw == "F":
001124:         return "M"
001125:     if raw == "S":
001126:         return "NB"
001127:     s = _normalize_text(value)
001128:     if s in {"H", "HOMBRE", "MASCULINO", "MALE"}:
001129:         return "H"
001130:     if s in {"M", "MUJER", "FEMENINO", "FEMALE"}:
001131:         return "M"
001132:     if s in {"NB", "NO BINARIO", "NO-BINARIO", "X"}:
001133:         return "NB"
001134:     return pd.NA

039. _to_ddmmyyyy
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA
LÍNEAS: 1137-1142
REFERENCIAS: NINGUNA
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
001137: def _to_ddmmyyyy(series: pd.Series, fallback: str | None = None) -> pd.Series:
001138:     dt = pd.to_datetime(series, errors="coerce", dayfirst=True)
001139:     out = dt.dt.strftime("%d/%m/%Y")
001140:     if fallback is not None:
001141:         out = out.fillna(fallback)
001142:     return out

040. _load_datos_alumnos_lookup

001145: def _load_datos_alumnos_lookup(input_file: Path) -> pd.DataFrame:
001146:     """Carga lookup por CODCLI desde hoja DatosAlumnos para gobernanza v2.
001147: 
001148:     El lookup se usa solo cuando el flag --usar-gobernanza-v2 está activo.
001149:     """
001150:     try:
001151:         xls = pd.ExcelFile(input_file)
001152:         if "DatosAlumnos" not in xls.sheet_names:
001153:             return pd.DataFrame()
001154: 
001155:         src = pd.read_excel(input_file, sheet_name="DatosAlumnos")
001156:         if "CODCLI" not in src.columns:
001157:             return pd.DataFrame()
001158: 
001159:         keep = [
001160:             "CODCLI",
001161:             "RUT",
001162:             "DIG",
001163:             "DV",
001164:             "NACIONALIDAD",
001165:             "SEDE",
001166:             "NOMBRES",
001167:             "APELLIDO PATERNO",
001168:             "APELLIDO MATERNO",
001169:             "SEXO",
001170:             "FECHANACIMIENTO",
001171:             "ANOINGRESO",
001172:             "PERIODOINGRESO",
001173:             "ANOMATRICULA",
001174:             "PERIODOMATRICULA",
001175:             "FECHAMATRICULA",
001176:             "NIVEL",
001177:             "SITUACION",
001178:             "ESTADOACADEMICO",
001179:             "MATRICULA",
001180:             "CON_FIRMA",
001181:             "COMUNACOLEGIO",
001182:             "CIUDADCOLEGIO",
001183:             "VIASDEADMISION",
001184:         ]
001185:         cols = [c for c in keep if c in src.columns]
001186:         out = src[cols].copy()
001187:         out["CODCLI"] = out["CODCLI"].astype(str).str.strip()
001188:         out = out[out["CODCLI"] != ""]
001189: 
001190:         rename_map = {
001191:             "RUT": "DA_RUT",
001192:             "DIG": "DA_DIG",
001193:             "DV": "DA_DV",
001194:             "NACIONALIDAD": "DA_NACIONALIDAD",
001195:             "SEDE": "DA_SEDE",
001196:             "NOMBRES": "DA_NOMBRES",
001197:             "APELLIDO PATERNO": "DA_APELLIDO_PATERNO",
001198:             "APELLIDO MATERNO": "DA_APELLIDO_MATERNO",
001199:             "SEXO": "DA_SEXO",
001200:             "FECHANACIMIENTO": "DA_FECHANACIMIENTO",
001201:             "ANOINGRESO": "DA_ANOINGRESO",
001202:             "PERIODOINGRESO": "DA_PERIODOINGRESO",
001203:             "ANOMATRICULA": "DA_ANOMATRICULA",
001204:             "PERIODOMATRICULA": "DA_PERIODOMATRICULA",
001205:             "FECHAMATRICULA": "DA_FECHAMATRICULA",
001206:             "NIVEL": "DA_NIVEL",
001207:             "SITUACION": "DA_SITUACION",
001208:             "ESTADOACADEMICO": "DA_ESTADOACADEMICO",
001209:             "MATRICULA": "DA_MATRICULA",
001210:             "CON_FIRMA": "DA_CON_FIRMA",
001211:             "COMUNACOLEGIO": "DA_COMUNACOLEGIO",
001212:             "CIUDADCOLEGIO": "DA_CIUDADCOLEGIO",
001213:             "VIASDEADMISION": "DA_VIASDEADMISION",
001214:         }
001215:         out = out.rename(columns=rename_map)
001216: 
001217:         if "DA_DV" not in out.columns and "DA_DIG" in out.columns:
001218:             out["DA_DV"] = out["DA_DIG"]
001219:         if "DA_DIG" not in out.columns and "DA_DV" in out.columns:
001220:             out["DA_DIG"] = out["DA_DV"]
001221: 
001222:         # Si no existe DA_DV ni DA_DIG, extraer del RUT (formato "12345678-K")
001223:         if "DA_DV" not in out.columns and "DA_RUT" in out.columns:
001224:             rut_str = out["DA_RUT"].astype(str).str.strip()
001225:             out["DA_DV"] = rut_str.str.split("-").str[-1].str.strip()
001226:             out["DA_RUT_NUM"] = rut_str.str.split("-").str[0].str.strip()
001227: 
001228:         if "DA_RUT_NORM" not in out.columns:
001229:             if "DA_RUT_NUM" in out.columns:
001230:                 out["DA_RUT_NORM"] = [_normalize_doc(n, d) for n, d in zip(out["DA_RUT_NUM"], out["DA_DV"])]
001231:             elif "DA_DV" in out.columns:
001232:                 out["DA_RUT_NORM"] = [_normalize_doc(n, d) for n, d in zip(out["DA_RUT"], out["DA_DV"])]
001233:             else:
001234:                 out["DA_RUT_NORM"] = ""
001235: 
001236:         out["DA_MATCH_FLAG"] = "1"
001237: 
001238:         # DatosAlumnos debería tener 1 fila por CODCLI; ante conflicto, conservar primera.
001239:         out = out.drop_duplicates(subset=["CODCLI"], keep="first").reset_index(drop=True)
001240:         return out
001241:     except Exception:
001242:         return pd.DataFrame()

====================================================================================================
041. _build_bridge_codcarpr_to_codcar
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 1252-1263
REFERENCIAS: CODCARPR
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
001252: def _build_bridge_codcarpr_to_codcar(df_bridge: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[str, int]:
001253:     """Construye mapa CODCARPR → CODIGO_CARRERA desde el puente SIES."""
001254:     if df_bridge.empty or "CODIGO_CARRERA" not in df_bridge.columns:
001255:         return {}
001256:     mapping: dict[str, int] = {}
001257:     work = df_bridge.copy()
001258:     work["CODIGO_CARRERA_NUM"] = pd.to_numeric(work["CODIGO_CARRERA"], errors="coerce")
001259:     for _, row in work.dropna(subset=["CODIGO_CARRERA_NUM"]).iterrows():
001260:         codcarpr = str(row.get("CODCARPR", "")).strip()
001261:         if codcarpr:
001262:             mapping[codcarpr] = int(row["CODIGO_CARRERA_NUM"])
001263:     return mapping

042. _build_revision_manual
CLASIFICACIÓN: LECTURA_PROMEDIOS
LÍNEAS: 1266-1329
REFERENCIAS: CODCARPR | DatosAlumnos
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
001266: def _build_revision_manual(
001267:     archivo_subida: pd.DataFrame,
001268:     sin_match: pd.DataFrame,
001269:     sin_match_datos_alumnos_df: pd.DataFrame,
001270: ) -> pd.DataFrame:
001271:     """Construye hoja REVISION_MANUAL con resumen accionable de pendientes."""
001272:     rows: list[dict] = []
001273:     # --- Sección 1: PENDIENTE_GOBERNANZA (ambiguos sin resolver) ---
001274:     pend = archivo_subida[archivo_subida.get("SIES_RESOLUCION_HEURISTICA", pd.Series()) == "PENDIENTE_GOBERNANZA"]
001275:     if not pend.empty:
001276:         for combo, grp in pend.groupby("CODIGOS_SIES_POTENCIALES", dropna=False):
001277:             codes = [c.strip() for c in str(combo).split("|")] if pd.notna(combo) else []
001278:             cod_cars = set()
001279:             for c in codes:
001280:                 m = _SIES_CODE_RE.match(c)
001281:                 if m:
001282:                     cod_cars.add(int(m.group("cod_car")))
001283:             mismo_cod = len(cod_cars) <= 1
001284:             rows.append({
001285:                 "SECCION": "PENDIENTE_GOBERNANZA",
001286:                 "PRIORIDAD": "BAJA (misma carrera, distinta versión)" if mismo_cod else "ALTA (carreras distintas)",
001287:                 "CODIGOS_SIES_POTENCIALES": combo,
001288:                 "CODIGO_CARRERA_OPCIONES": " | ".join(str(c) for c in sorted(cod_cars)) if cod_cars else "",
001289:                 "N_ALUMNOS_AFECTADOS": len(grp),
001290:                 "CODCARPR_EJEMPLO": grp.iloc[0].get("CODCARPR_NORM", grp.iloc[0].get("COD_CAR", "")),
001291:                 "NOMBRE_CARRERA_EJEMPLO": grp.iloc[0].get("NOMBRE_CARRERA_FUENTE", ""),
001292:                 "ACCION_REQUERIDA": "Definir versión correcta en gobernanza" if mismo_cod else "REVISAR: definir carrera correcta",
001293:             })
001294:     # --- Sección 2: SIN_MATCH_SIES ---
001295:     if not sin_match.empty:
001296:         codcarpr_col = "CODCARPR_NORM" if "CODCARPR_NORM" in sin_match.columns else "COD_CAR"
001297:         for codcarpr, grp in sin_match.groupby(codcarpr_col, dropna=False):
001298:             rows.append({
001299:                 "SECCION": "SIN_MATCH_SIES",
001300:                 "PRIORIDAD": "ALTA (sin código SIES)",
001301:                 "CODIGOS_SIES_POTENCIALES": "",
001302:                 "CODIGO_CARRERA_OPCIONES": "",
001303:                 "N_ALUMNOS_AFECTADOS": len(grp),
001304:                 "CODCARPR_EJEMPLO": codcarpr,
001305:                 "NOMBRE_CARRERA_EJEMPLO": grp.iloc[0].get("NOMBRE_CARRERA_FUENTE", ""),
001306:                 "ACCION_REQUERIDA": "Agregar mapeo SIES para este CODCARPR",
001307:             })
001308:     # --- Sección 3: SIN_MATCH_DATOS_ALUMNOS (resumen) ---
001309:     if not sin_match_datos_alumnos_df.empty:
001310:         for codcarpr, grp in sin_match_datos_alumnos_df.groupby("COD_CAR_FUENTE", dropna=False):
001311:             rows.append({
001312:                 "SECCION": "SIN_MATCH_DATOS_ALUMNOS",
001313:                 "PRIORIDAD": "MEDIA (sin match en DatosAlumnos)",
001314:                 "CODIGOS_SIES_POTENCIALES": "",
001315:                 "CODIGO_CARRERA_OPCIONES": "",
001316:                 "N_ALUMNOS_AFECTADOS": len(grp),
001317:                 "CODCARPR_EJEMPLO": codcarpr,
001318:                 "NOMBRE_CARRERA_EJEMPLO": grp.iloc[0].get("NOMBRE_CARRERA_FUENTE", ""),
001319:                 "ACCION_REQUERIDA": "Verificar si alumnos existen en hoja DatosAlumnos",
001320:             })
001321:     if not rows:
001322:         return pd.DataFrame(columns=[
001323:             "SECCION", "PRIORIDAD", "CODIGOS_SIES_POTENCIALES",
001324:             "CODIGO_CARRERA_OPCIONES", "N_ALUMNOS_AFECTADOS",
001325:             "CODCARPR_EJEMPLO", "NOMBRE_CARRERA_EJEMPLO", "ACCION_REQUERIDA",
001326:         ])
001327:     result = pd.DataFrame(rows)
001328:     result = result.sort_values(["SECCION", "N_ALUMNOS_AFECTADOS"], ascending=[True, False]).reset_index(drop=True)
001329:     return result

043. _write_excel_atomic
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 1332-1369
REFERENCIAS: CODCLI
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
001332: def _write_excel_atomic(
001333:     sheets: dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
001334:     final_path: Path,
001335:     red_rows_sheet: str | None = None,
001336:     red_rows_mask: pd.Series | None = None,
001337: ) -> None:
001338:     from openpyxl.styles import PatternFill, Font
001339:     final_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
001340:     with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="mu_export_") as tmpdir:
001341:         tmp_path = Path(tmpdir) / final_path.name
001342:         with pd.ExcelWriter(tmp_path, engine="openpyxl") as writer:
001343:             for sheet_name, df in sheets.items():
001344:                 df.to_excel(writer, index=False, sheet_name=sheet_name[:31])
001345:             # ── Formato rojo en CODCLI para filas que requieren revisión manual ──
001346:             if red_rows_sheet and red_rows_mask is not None and red_rows_mask.any():
001347:                 ws = writer.sheets[red_rows_sheet[:31]]
001348:                 red_fill = PatternFill(start_color="FFC7CE", end_color="FFC7CE", fill_type="solid")
001349:                 red_font = Font(color="9C0006")
001350:                 df_target = sheets[red_rows_sheet]
001351:                 codcli_col_idx = list(df_target.columns).index("CODCLI") + 1 if "CODCLI" in df_target.columns else 1
001352:                 # Columnas clave para marcar: CODCLI, N_CODES_SIES, SIES_RESOLUCION_HEURISTICA
001353:                 mark_cols = []
001354:                 for cname in ["CODCLI", "N_CODES_SIES", "CODIGOS_SIES_POTENCIALES", "SIES_RESOLUCION_HEURISTICA"]:
001355:                     if cname in df_target.columns:
001356:                         mark_cols.append(list(df_target.columns).index(cname) + 1)
001357:                 if not mark_cols:
001358:                     mark_cols = [codcli_col_idx]
001359:                 pos_map = {v: i for i, v in enumerate(df_target.index)}
001360:                 for idx in red_rows_mask[red_rows_mask].index:
001361:                     if idx in pos_map:
001362:                         excel_row = pos_map[idx] + 2
001363:                         for col in mark_cols:
001364:                             cell = ws.cell(row=excel_row, column=col)
001365:                             cell.fill = red_fill
001366:                             cell.font = red_font
001367:         if final_path.exists():
001368:             final_path.unlink()
001369:         shutil.copy2(tmp_path, final_path)

045. _consolidar_candidatos_por_codcli
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 1396-1504
REFERENCIAS: CODCLI
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
001396: def _consolidar_candidatos_por_codcli(
001397:     candidatos: pd.DataFrame,
001398:     estado_carga: pd.Series,
001399: ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.Series, pd.DataFrame]:
001400:     """Consolidación de candidatos usando CODCLI como clave interna primaria.
001401: 
001402:     Paso 1 – Intra-CODCLI (Caso A): si un mismo CODCLI tiene >1 fila,
001403:              se queda la de FECHA_MATRICULA más reciente.
001404:     Paso 2 – Clave compuesta 8-col (red de seguridad legacy):
001405:              dedup por [TIPO_DOC, N_DOC, DV, COD_SED, COD_CAR, MODALIDAD, JOR, VERSION].
001406:     Paso 3 – Multi-identidad (Caso C): clasifica sin eliminar.
001407:              Multi-carrera legítima se MANTIENE per manual.
001408: 
001409:     Retorna (candidatos_consolidados, estado_carga, auditoría).
001410:     """
001411:     audit_rows: list[dict] = []
001412:     identity_keys = ["TIPO_DOC", "N_DOC", "DV"]
001413: 
001414:     # ── Paso 1: Intra-CODCLI ──────────────────────────────────────────
001415:     # Tie-breaker: cuando _FECHA_MAT_TMP empata (ej. sentinel 01/01/1900),
001416:     # preferir fila con NIV_ACA más alto (nivel más avanzado).
001417:     _niv_sort = pd.to_numeric(candidatos.get("NIV_ACA"), errors="coerce").fillna(0)
001418:     candidatos["_NIV_SORT"] = _niv_sort
001419:     candidatos = candidatos.sort_values(
001420:         ["CODCLI", "_FECHA_MAT_TMP", "_NIV_SORT"], ascending=[True, False, False],
001421:     )
001422:     intra_dup = candidatos.duplicated(subset=["CODCLI"], keep="first")
001423:     for idx in candidatos.index[intra_dup]:
001424:         r = candidatos.loc[idx]
001425:         audit_rows.append({
001426:             "CODCLI": r["CODCLI"], "TIPO_DOC": r["TIPO_DOC"],
001427:             "N_DOC": r["N_DOC"], "DV": r["DV"],
001428:             "COD_CAR": r["COD_CAR"],
001429:             "NOMBRE_CARRERA": r.get("NOMBRE_CARRERA_FUENTE", ""),
001430:             "VIG": r["VIG"], "ANIO_ING_ACT": r.get("ANIO_ING_ACT", ""),
001431:             "CASO": "A_INTRA_CODCLI",
001432:             "CLASIFICACION": "DUPLICADO_TECNICO",
001433:             "ACCION": "EXCLUIDO",
001434:         })
001435:     estado_carga.loc[candidatos.index[intra_dup]] = "EXCLUIDO_DUPLICADO_INTRA_CODCLI"
001436:     candidatos = candidatos.loc[~intra_dup].copy()
001437:     candidatos.drop(columns=["_NIV_SORT"], inplace=True, errors="ignore")
001438: 
001439:     # ── Paso 2: Dedup clave compuesta 8-col (legacy safety-net) ───────
001440:     dedupe_keys = [
001441:         "TIPO_DOC", "N_DOC", "DV", "COD_SED",
001442:         "COD_CAR", "MODALIDAD", "JOR", "VERSION",
001443:     ]
001444:     candidatos = candidatos.sort_values(
001445:         ["TIPO_DOC", "N_DOC", "DV", "COD_CAR", "_FECHA_MAT_TMP"],
001446:         ascending=[True, True, True, True, False],
001447:     )
001448:     dup_8col = candidatos.duplicated(subset=dedupe_keys, keep="first")
001449:     for idx in candidatos.index[dup_8col]:
001450:         r = candidatos.loc[idx]
001451:         audit_rows.append({
001452:             "CODCLI": r["CODCLI"], "TIPO_DOC": r["TIPO_DOC"],
001453:             "N_DOC": r["N_DOC"], "DV": r["DV"],
001454:             "COD_CAR": r["COD_CAR"],
001455:             "NOMBRE_CARRERA": r.get("NOMBRE_CARRERA_FUENTE", ""),
001456:             "VIG": r["VIG"], "ANIO_ING_ACT": r.get("ANIO_ING_ACT", ""),
001457:             "CASO": "B_DUPLICADO_CLAVE_CARGA",
001458:             "CLASIFICACION": "DUPLICADO_CLAVE_8_COL",
001459:             "ACCION": "EXCLUIDO",
001460:         })
001461:     estado_carga.loc[candidatos.index[dup_8col]] = "EXCLUIDO_DUPLICADO_CLAVE_CARGA"
001462:     candidatos = candidatos.loc[~dup_8col].copy()
001463: 
001464:     # ── Paso 3: Clasificar multi-identidad (Caso C) ──────────────────
001465:     id_counts = candidatos.groupby(identity_keys, sort=False).size()
001466:     multi_ids = id_counts[id_counts > 1]
001467: 
001468:     for keys in multi_ids.index:
001469:         tipo, ndoc, dv = keys
001470:         mask = (
001471:             (candidatos["TIPO_DOC"] == tipo)
001472:             & (candidatos["N_DOC"] == ndoc)
001473:             & (candidatos["DV"] == dv)
001474:         )
001475:         group = candidatos.loc[mask]
001476:         vigs = set(group["VIG"].astype(int).unique())
001477: 
001478:         if vigs == {1}:
001479:             clasificacion = "MULTI_CARRERA_AMBAS_VIGENTES"
001480:         elif vigs == {2}:
001481:             clasificacion = "MULTI_CARRERA_AMBAS_EGRESADAS"
001482:         elif vigs.issuperset({1, 2}):
001483:             clasificacion = "MULTI_CARRERA_MIXTA_VIG1_VIG2"
001484:         else:
001485:             clasificacion = "MULTI_CARRERA_OTRO"
001486: 
001487:         for _, r in group.iterrows():
001488:             audit_rows.append({
001489:                 "CODCLI": r["CODCLI"], "TIPO_DOC": r["TIPO_DOC"],
001490:                 "N_DOC": r["N_DOC"], "DV": r["DV"],
001491:                 "COD_CAR": r["COD_CAR"],
001492:                 "NOMBRE_CARRERA": r.get("NOMBRE_CARRERA_FUENTE", ""),
001493:                 "VIG": r["VIG"], "ANIO_ING_ACT": r.get("ANIO_ING_ACT", ""),
001494:                 "CASO": "C_MULTI_CODCLI_MISMA_IDENTIDAD",
001495:                 "CLASIFICACION": clasificacion,
001496:                 "ACCION": "MANTENER_MULTI_CARRERA",
001497:             })
001498: 
001499:     auditoria = (
001500:         pd.DataFrame(audit_rows, columns=_AUDIT_CONSOL_COLUMNS)
001501:         if audit_rows
001502:         else pd.DataFrame(columns=_AUDIT_CONSOL_COLUMNS)
001503:     )
001504:     return candidatos, estado_carga, auditoria

046. _load_governance_tsv
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA
LÍNEAS: 1524-1545
REFERENCIAS: NINGUNA
LLAMA A: NINGUNA
LLAMADA DESDE: _load_for_ing_act_catalog | ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
001524: def _load_governance_tsv(path: str | None, required_columns: list[str]) -> pd.DataFrame:
001525:     """Carga TSV de gobernanza desde archivo externo.
001526: 
001527:     Si no existe archivo o faltan columnas obligatorias, retorna DataFrame vacío.
001528:     """
001529:     if not path:
001530:         return pd.DataFrame()
001531: 
001532:     p = Path(path).expanduser().resolve()
001533:     if not p.exists():
001534:         return pd.DataFrame()
001535: 
001536:     try:
001537:         df = pd.read_csv(p, sep="\t", dtype=str, keep_default_na=False)
001538:     except Exception:
001539:         return pd.DataFrame()
001540: 
001541:     missing = [c for c in required_columns if c not in df.columns]
001542:     if missing:
001543:         return pd.DataFrame()
001544: 
001545:     return df.copy()

047. _normalize_code_or_na
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA

LLAMADA DESDE: ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like

001548: def _normalize_code_or_na(value: object) -> object:
001549:     if pd.isna(value):
001550:         return pd.NA
001551:     text = str(value).strip()
001552:     return text if text else pd.NA

048. _extract_alpha_prefix
CLASIFICACIÓN: DEPENDENCIA_TECNICA
LÍNEAS: 1555-1558
REFERENCIAS: NINGUNA
LLAMA A: _normalize_text
LLAMADA DESDE: _build_catalog_and_bridge_from_duracion
====================================================================================================
001555: def _extract_alpha_prefix(value: object) -> str:
001556:     text = _normalize_text(value)
001557:     m = re.match(r"^([A-Z]+)", text)
001558:     return m.group(1) if m else ""

====================================================================================================
049. _build_key_3
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 1561-1562
REFERENCIAS: CODCARPR
LLAMA A: _normalize_text
LLAMADA DESDE: _prepare_catalog_manual | _prepare_puente_sies
====================================================================================================
001561: def _build_key_3(jornada: object, codcarpr: object, nombre: object) -> str:
001562:     return f"{_normalize_text(jornada)}|{_normalize_text(codcarpr)}|{_normalize_text(nombre)}"

====================================================================================================

====================================================================================================
051. _prepare_catalog_manual
CLASIFICACIÓN: LLAVES_Y_CRUCES
LÍNEAS: 1585-1603
REFERENCIAS: CODCARPR
LLAMA A: _build_key_3 | _build_key_no_jornada
LLAMADA DESDE: _build_catalog_and_bridge_from_duracion | ejecutar_pipeline_matricula_unificada_legacy_like
====================================================================================================
001585: def _prepare_catalog_manual(df: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
001586:     if df.empty:
001587:         return df
001588: 
001589:     required = {"GRUPO_TRAZA", "JORNADA", "CODCARPR", "NOMBRE_L"}
001590:     missing = required - set(df.columns)
001591:     if missing:
001592:         raise ValueError(f"Catálogo manual inválido: faltan columnas {sorted(missing)}")
001593: 
001594:     out = df.copy()
001595:     out["GRUPO_TRAZA"] = out["GRUPO_TRAZA"].astype(str).str.strip()
001596:     out["JORNADA"] = out["JORNADA"].map(_normalize_text)
001597:     out["CODCARPR"] = out["CODCARPR"].map(_normalize_text)
001598:     out["NOMBRE_L"] = out["NOMBRE_L"].map(_normalize_text)
001599:     out["FAMILIA_TRAZA"] = out["GRUPO_TRAZA"].map(_extract_alpha_prefix)
001600:     out["FAMILIA_CODCARPR"] = out["CODCARPR"].map(_extract_alpha_prefix)
001601:     out["MANUAL_KEY_3"] = out.apply(lambda r: _build_key_3(r["JORNADA"], r["CODCARPR"], r["NOMBRE_L"]), axis=1)
001602:     out["MANUAL_KEY_NO_JORNADA"] = out.apply(lambda r: _build_key_no_jornada(r["CODCARPR"], r["NOMBRE_L"]), axis=1)
001603:     return out.drop_duplicates().reset_index(drop=True)
